						limit = {

						}

					}

						limit = { 
							has_global_flag = USA_the_bill_failed
						}
						country_event = USA.13 ##《加纳-瓦格纳法案》未能通过！
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

						bonus = 1
					}

					has_completed_focus = USA_grand_navy
				}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
